# Rasm disagreements — full listing

Every word where the riwāyāt disagree about the letters on the line, in order. Dots, hamzah and vowelling have all been set aside, and so has the dagger alif — a superscript alef is by definition an alef the scribe did not write on the line.

The 842 `rasm_variant` words come first: a letter one riwāyah has and another does not, splitting the riwāyāt 62 different ways. The 197 `alif_variant` words follow: one skeleton once every ā is spelled out, differing only in where the ā was written, and splitting the KFGQPC riwāyāt exactly one way. See *The ā on the line or above it* in `COMPARISON.md` for why that difference is reported apart.

Machine-readable: `../differences.csv`, `../differences.json`.
